To generate a summary of a review, we'll follow these steps:

1.  Import the necessary libraries.

3.  Remove stop words to reduce noise in the text.
4.  Lemmatize the words to their base

from nltk.corpus import stopwords
from nltk.stem import WordNetLemmatizer
from nltk.tokenize import word_tokenize

# Initialize NLTK
